Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The spectrum variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 80 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 215 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 80 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spectrum structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 215. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
